Ref. No. DFC (COC) 49/ED(0)-84.—^In exercise of the 
powers conferred by sections 45J, 45K and 45L of the Reserve 
Bank of India Act, 1934 (2 of 1934) and of all the powers 
enabling it in this. behalf, the Reserve Bank of India being 
satisfied that it is ncce.,'yaiy in the public interest so to do, 
hereby directs that the Non-Bonking Financial Companiesi 
(Reserve Bank) Directions, 1977 shall, with immediate effect 
be amended in the following manner, namely— 

1. In paragraph 11, for the words “two per cent”, the 
words “one per cent” shall be substituted. 

2. For the existing paragraph 12 in PART HI, the 
following shall bo substituted namely— 

12. Maintenance of a minimum percentage of liquid assets 

Every hire-purchase finance company and every housing 
finance company shall maintair in India ; (a) in an account 
with a scheduled bank (free from any charge or lien) or 
(b) In the unencumbered approved securities (such securities 
being valued at their market rate for the time being) or 
partly in such an account or partly in such securities, a sum 
which shall not at close of business on any day ten per 
cent of the deposits outstanding in the books of the company 
on that day. 

Explanation 

For the purpose of this paragraph : 

(a) “approved securities” means securities in which a 
trustee may invest money under clause (a).

INSTRUCTIONS FOR FILLING IN THE RETURN 

1. The return should be submitted by a non-banking financial company covered by Notification No. DNBC. 3^/DGtI-l)-77 dated 
the 20th June 1977 and by a miscellaneous non-banking company covered by Notification No. DNBC. 39/1:G (H)-77 dated the

the financial year of the company concerned. 

2. Thi submission of the Feturn should not be delayed for any reasons such as the finalisation/complction of tlic ei dit of tht t r 11 al 
accounts. The compilation of the return should be on the basis of the figures available in the books of account of the company. 

3. The number of accounts should be given in actual figures while the amounts of deposits should be given in thousands of rupees 
Amount should be rounded off to the nearest thousand. For example, an amount of Rs. 4*560 should he shown as 5 and hi t as 
4 -6 or 5000. Similarly an amount of Rs. 61,495 is to be shown as 61 and not as 61 *4 or 61000, 

4. The p^riodwiso classification of deposits should be made against the various heads under its No. 7 of Paul 1 of the rcii i n : (u ' d rg 
to the periods for which they have been originally rec^ivcd/last renewed and not according to the periods they h»^ r to i vn u.h f j t m 
the 31st March i.e. the date of the return.
